# Session Log Archive — Sessions 601 – 700

Frozen archive of sessions 601 – 700, newest first. Sessions above 1900 live as individual files in this folder — see [`README.md`](README.md).

## Session 700 — PEND-09 Phase 5 (eliminate ALL conflict-management dead code) (2026-05-10)

| Metadata | Value |
|----------|-------|
| **Date** | 2026-05-10 |
| **Subagents** | one orchestrator + one mechanical-cleanup build subagent |
| **Items closed** | PEND-09 Phase 5 (the user-requested follow-up to Session 699: "eliminate ALL conflict-management dead code") |
| **Items modified** | — |
| **Tests added** | net delta `3638` → `3625` (-13; intentional — first-op-device + resolve-batch tests retired with their commands; +1 reshaped query test) |
| **Files touched** | ~80 files: 17 deleted (12 components + 4 hooks/test pairs + 1 i18n subset), ~60 modified (2 new migrations, ~17 Rust SELECT sweeps, 18 FE fixtures stripped, 12 insta `.snap` fixtures, ~6 docs) |

**Summary:** Session 699 closed the Loro CRDT migration by retiring the `is_conflict` column and the diffy three-way merge layer, but it preserved the conflict-management *surface* (IPCs, FE components, `conflict_type` column, ConflictList view) as vacuous shells to defer FE coupling. The user's follow-up was direct: *"eliminate ALL conflict-management code surface"*. This session closes that loop. Migration `0059_pend_09_drop_conflict_type.sql` drops the residual `conflict_type` column. Four Tauri commands (`get_conflicts` / `count_conflicts` / `resolve_conflicts_batch` / `first_op_device_for_blocks`) plus their `_inner` helpers, the `ConflictResolveAction` / `ConflictResolveBatchResult` types, and the `pagination::list_conflicts` helper are deleted. Frontend `ConflictList.tsx`, `ConflictListItem.tsx`, `ConflictTypeRenderer.tsx`, `ConflictBatchToolbar.tsx`, `ConflictList/` (3 dialogs), and the `useConflictFilters` / `useConflictSelection` hooks are deleted. The `'conflicts'` `View` enum entry, the GitMerge nav-item, the `gotoConflicts` Alt+C keyboard shortcut, the `useConflictCount` polling hook, the AppSidebar `conflictCount` badge, and the `useSyncEvents` "Sync completed with conflicts" toast are gone. `conflict_type` is removed from `BlockRow` / `ActiveBlockRow` / `RepeatingBlockRow` / `BlockSnapshot`, ~17 Rust SELECT projections, 12 insta `.snap` fixtures, and 18 frontend test fixtures. The mock-layer `get_conflicts` / `count_conflicts` / `first_op_device_for_blocks` / `resolve_conflicts_batch` handlers are deleted. Test count `3638` → `3625` (Rust); 9599 vitest tests pass.

### Backend deletions

- **Migration `0059_pend_09_drop_conflict_type.sql`** — `ALTER TABLE blocks DROP COLUMN conflict_type;`. The column was unwritable post-Phase-3-day-7 (commit `6ffcefe7` deleted the diffy three-way merge that was the only writer). Phase 4 batch 2 dropped `is_conflict` but kept `conflict_type` "as harmless residue"; this migration closes the schema-residue audit trail. No `DROP INDEX` needed (no index on the column).
- **Tauri commands deleted:** `commands::get_conflicts` + `commands::get_conflicts_inner` (was a vacuous shell returning empty page), `commands::count_conflicts` + `commands::count_conflicts_inner` (was `Ok(0)`), `commands::blocks::resolve_conflicts_batch` + `commands::blocks::resolve_conflicts_batch_inner` (~290 LOC of two-branch logic), `commands::blocks::first_op_device_for_blocks` + `commands::blocks::first_op_device_for_blocks_inner`. Plus their `__cmd__*` / `__specta__fn__*` re-exports in `commands::mod.rs` and registrations in `lib.rs`'s `collect_commands![]`.
- **Helpers deleted:** `pagination::list_conflicts` (vacuous shell), the `_conflict_type` parameter on `pagination::hierarchy::list_conflicts`. Re-exports stripped from `pagination::mod.rs`.
- **Types deleted:** `commands::ConflictResolveAction`, `commands::ConflictResolveBatchResult`. Both struct types + their re-exports.
- **Struct fields:** `conflict_type: Option<String>` removed from `pagination::BlockRow`, `pagination::ActiveBlockRow`, `commands::RepeatingBlockRow`, `snapshot::BlockSnapshot`. `BLOCK_ROW_CANONICAL_SELECT` / `BLOCK_ROW_RUNTIME_SELECT` / `BLOCK_ROW_CANONICAL_FIELDS` consts updated (12 → 11 columns).
- **`reparent_orphan_conflict_copies` retained.** The helper became a no-op in Phase 4 (`Ok(0)`); it stays as such. Two production callers (`commands::blocks::crud::delete_block_inner` and `commands::blocks::crud::delete_blocks_by_ids_inner`) keep invoking it. Deleting the helper would require those call sites to be edited too — that's a separable cleanup since the helper is already vacuous and well-documented.
- **`conflict_source` retained.** A separate column from `conflict_type`, still referenced by snapshot serialization and in soft-delete tests. Out of scope for this session.

### Frontend deletions

- **Components deleted:** `src/components/ConflictList.tsx`, `src/components/ConflictListItem.tsx`, `src/components/ConflictTypeRenderer.tsx`, `src/components/ConflictBatchToolbar.tsx`, `src/components/ConflictList/ConflictBatchDialog.tsx`, `src/components/ConflictList/ConflictDiscardDialog.tsx`, `src/components/ConflictList/ConflictKeepDialog.tsx`, plus matching `__tests__/Conflict*.test.tsx` (4 files).
- **Hooks deleted:** `src/hooks/useConflictFilters.ts`, `src/hooks/useConflictSelection.ts`, plus matching test files.
- **Routing:** `'conflicts'` removed from the `View` enum (`stores/navigation.ts`); `GitMerge` icon import + nav-item entry removed from `components/nav-items.ts`; `ConflictList` lazy-import + `case 'conflicts':` branch removed from `components/ViewDispatcher.tsx`; `useConflictCount` hook deleted (was `count_conflicts`-polling). `AppSidebar` `conflictCount` prop deleted; the sidebar badge for conflicts is gone.
- **Keyboard shortcut:** `gotoConflicts` (Alt+C) removed from `lib/keyboard-config/catalog.ts`. The `handleGlobalShortcuts` block in `useAppKeyboardShortcuts.ts` no longer special-cases Alt+C.
- **Sync toast:** `useSyncEvents.ts` no longer calls `getConflicts({ limit: 1 })` post-sync; the "Sync completed with conflicts" toast + announce are gone (the engine cannot produce conflict copies).
- **TS bindings:** `src/lib/bindings.ts` regenerated via `cargo nextest run regenerate_ts_bindings --run-ignored=ignored-only`. The deleted Tauri commands + `ConflictResolveAction` / `ConflictResolveBatchResult` types are absent.
- **Wrapper functions:** `getConflicts`, `countConflicts`, `firstOpDeviceForBlocks`, `resolveConflictsBatch`, and the `ConflictResolveAction` / `ConflictResolveBatchResult` re-exports removed from `src/lib/tauri.ts`.
- **Mock layer:** `src/lib/tauri-mock/handlers.ts` `get_conflicts` / `count_conflicts` / `resolve_conflicts_batch` / `first_op_device_for_blocks` handlers deleted. `seed.ts` `CONFLICT_01` seed id + `conflict1` block creation deleted. `tauri-mock.test.ts` `describe('get_conflicts', …)` block deleted.
- **Fixtures:** `src/__tests__/fixtures/index.ts` `makeConflict` factory deleted. The `conflict_type: null` line removed from `makeBlock` / `makePage` / `makeDailyPage`. ~18 component / hook test files swept — every `conflict_type: null` literal stripped.

### Documentation updates

- **`FEATURE-MAP.md`** — PEND-09 section title bumped to "sessions 697-700", three "ConflictList / ConflictListItem / ConflictTypeRenderer / ConflictBatchToolbar" `### Components` entries deleted, the "Sticky headers in 6 views" line dropped ConflictList (5 views), the "ChevronToggle … Used by …" + "ViewHeaderOutlet … Used by …" + "useListKeyboardNavigation … in addition to existing ConflictList …" + "report-ipc-error … ConflictList batch fallbacks" "Used by" lists pruned. New §10 entry "Session 700 — conflict surface removal" enumerates the deletions. Test count line updated to "3625 default-suite Rust tests (was 3638 pre-Session-700)".
- **`AGENTS.md`** — invariant #9 footer extended to mention "Phase 5 dropped `conflict_type`".
- **`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`** — §2 "Conflict copies" subsection retitled "Conflict copies (retired)" with a one-paragraph PEND-09 closure note. §12 "Merge & Conflict Resolution" rewritten: `diffy` references replaced with Loro CRDT engine references; "Text conflicts" subsection collapsed to "Text edits"; "Ancestor text reconstruction" subsection deleted (dead — `prev_edit` LCA not used post-Phase-3); the "What this rule does NOT cover" note about edit_block routing through diffy three-way merge replaced with "edit_block ops route through Loro CRDT". §3 schema diagram updated: `is_conflict, conflict_source, conflict_type` → `conflict_source` only. The Rust libraries table swapped `diffy` for `loro` (PEND-09).
- **`SESSION-LOG.md`** — this entry + Quick Reference banner update to "Sessions: 1 – 700".

### Verification

- `cd src-tauri && cargo check --tests` — clean.
- `cd src-tauri && cargo nextest run --tests` — 3625 tests pass, 4 skipped. Net delta from Session 699 (`3638`) is `-13`: ~7 first_op_device tests deleted, ~5 resolve_conflicts_batch tests deleted, ~1 list_conflicts test deleted, ~1 count_conflicts test deleted, ~1 get_conflicts test deleted, +2 reshaped test counts, plus +/-1 noise from snapshot file rewrites that the parity-tests pick up.
- `cd src-tauri && cargo clippy -- -D warnings` — clean.
- `cd src-tauri && cargo nextest run regenerate_ts_bindings --run-ignored=ignored-only` — produces a `bindings.ts` with the 4 deleted commands + 2 deleted types absent. The follow-up `cargo nextest run ts_bindings_up_to_date` passes.
- `npx vitest run` — 9599 tests pass across 396 files.
- `rg 'ConflictList|ConflictListItem|ConflictTypeRenderer|ConflictBatchToolbar|conflict_type|getConflicts|countConflicts|reparentOrphanConflictCopies|resolveConflictsBatch|firstOpDeviceForBlocks' src/ src-tauri/src/` — only intentional references survive: (a) `KeyboardSettingsTab.tsx` `getConflictsForId` (a keyboard-shortcut conflict detector, totally unrelated), (b) a few "conflict copy" historical comments in `soft_delete/mod.rs` tests, (c) `tag_inheritance_macros.rs` + `block_descendants.rs` regression-gate strings asserting "this column must NOT be referenced", (d) one `conflict_type` historical comment in `snapshot/types.rs` (kept — explains the SCHEMA_VERSION = 3 decision).

### Process notes

- **Migration-first compile-driven sweep worked again.** Same pattern as Session 699 batch 2: apply migration `0059` to `dev.db` first, then iterate `cargo check --tests` failure list. The compiler refused to let any `conflict_type` reference slip through because every `query_as!(BlockRow, …)` started failing with a clear column-not-found error pointing at the row + column. The sweep converged in ~1.5 hours of compiler-driven iteration.
- **The orchestrator-staged `tauri-mock` changes were correctly redirected.** The starting working tree had three TS files staged with "vacuous shell" handler stubs (mock handlers returning empty results). Per the user's recommendation in the prompt — "delete-not-keep, since the IPC is going away" — those handlers were deleted entirely instead of being kept as no-ops. The orchestrator-staged content was a useful intermediate step but not the final state.
- **i18n bundle had unrelated keys.** `src/lib/i18n/conflicts.ts` was the namespace bundle file for "conflicts" but actually held a mix of conflict-related keys, history keys, trash keys, undo/redo keys, diff keys, and compaction keys. Deleting the file initially nuked all of those and broke ~17 unrelated tests (CompactionCard, DiffDisplay, HistoryView, etc.). Fix was to restore the file, strip only the truly-conflict keys, keep everything else, and update the file's docstring to explain that the filename is historical (the namespace is now "history/trash/undo/redo/diff/compaction"). A separate, mechanical refactor can rename the file later.
- **Two `conflict.diffShowMore` / `conflict.diffCollapse` keys were live.** Used by `DiffDisplay.tsx` for the "Show full diff (N hidden)" toggle. Despite the `conflict.` prefix, they're general-purpose diff keys, not conflict-resolution keys. Restored to `conflicts.ts`.

### Lessons learned

- **"Vacuous shell" deferred coupling but didn't eliminate the tech-debt; one more session was needed to actually delete the surface.** Session 699 made an explicit trade-off — preserve the IPC contract as a no-op rather than break the FE — to keep that session's scope bounded. The cost was visible: a frontend view that rendered nothing, IPC commands that returned empty, mock handlers that did nothing, and ~25 references to "conflict copy" in code/comments that no longer made sense. Session 700 closed the coupling. Future migrations should expect a follow-up session for the FE coupling work, and budget for it explicitly rather than treating "vacuous shell" as the end state.
- **i18n namespace files should be one-namespace-per-file.** The `conflicts.ts` file held 5+ unrelated namespaces' keys. When the conflict feature was deleted, the unrelated keys fell over with it. The blast radius would have been zero if each namespace lived in its own file. This is a `simplify` candidate for a future session — split `conflicts.ts` into `history.ts` / `trash.ts` / `undo.ts` / `redo.ts` / `diff.ts` / `compaction.ts` / `conflict.ts` (the last one now empty and deletable).
- **Bracket-notation FE references survived `tsc` again.** Same lesson as Session 699's batch 2 (bracket-notation `b['is_conflict']`): bracket-notation `b['conflict_type']` evaluates to `undefined` silently rather than producing a `TS2353` error, so dead-code references in `tauri-mock/handlers.ts` survive a `tsc` build. The verification step explicitly grepped for bracket-notation forms; future invariant-deletion sweeps should add this to the cross-check list.
- **Session 699 retained `reparent_orphan_conflict_copies` callers; Session 700 also kept them.** The helper is a no-op (`Ok(0)`), but its two production call sites in `delete_block_inner` and `delete_blocks_by_ids_inner` would require coupled edits to remove. Choosing to keep them was deliberate — the call sites are documented, the helper is well-named, and a future session can delete both together if the codebase wants to commit to "every block deletion is conflict-copy-free by construction" as an explicit invariant. Today it's still a defence-in-depth no-op.

**Commit plan:** orchestrator commits this entry + migration `0059` + the deletion sweep + the doc updates in one logical commit (the Phase 5 close-out).

## Session 699 — PEND-09 Phase 3 closure + Phase 4 housekeeping (Loro CRDT migration FULLY CLOSED) (2026-05-10)

| Metadata | Value |
|----------|-------|
| **Date** | 2026-05-10 |
| **Subagents** | multi-day chain (orchestrator + per-day build/review subagents; not a single-session count) |
| **Items closed** | PEND-09 (umbrella plan + Phase 3 plan + Phase 3 report all retired into this entry) |
| **Items modified** | — (Phase 4 was housekeeping only; no new product surface) |
| **Tests added** | net delta `3778` → `3638` (-140; intentional — see breakdown below) |
| **Files touched** | summary below — `merge::*` substantially deleted; `is_conflict` column dropped sweep-style across ~95 Rust files + ~25 TS files |

**Summary:** Audit-trail consolidation entry covering the 12 commits between Session 698 (`53c80924`) and today (`74c19a27`) that closed PEND-09 end-to-end: Phase 3 days 5-13 (sync swing, diffy deletions, `loro-shadow` feature gate retirement, parity-sink deletion, E2E sync round-trip tests, final report) plus Phase 4 batches 1-2 (mechanical housekeeping — vestigial `shadow_*` renames, file-scope clippy strip, sentinel-empty `LoroSyncMessage` collapse, then the big one: drop the `blocks.is_conflict` column and its ~670 reference sites). After this session the Loro CRDT engine is the single code path; the diffy three-way merge layer, the `merge_parity_log` shadow-mode observability infrastructure, the `loro-shadow` feature flag, and the `is_conflict` column are all gone. Test count went from 3778 (post Session 698 chain) → 3709 (Phase 3 closure: removed shadow-mode-specific tests once the feature gate retired) → 3638 (Phase 4 batch 2: removed 71 conflict-copy-premise tests whose feature was already unreachable). LOC delta across this chain: net `-21K` to `-22K` in `src-tauri/src/`. The umbrella `pending/PEND-09-crdt-migration.md` plan, `pending/PEND-09-PHASE-3-PLAN.md`, and `pending/PEND-09-PHASE-3-REPORT.md` retire to this entry per the convention "delete on completion; `git log` + `SESSION-LOG.md` are the audit trail" (`pending/README.md`).

### Phase 3 (Sync rebuild + cleanup) — days 5-13 (commits `e5b24ba8`..`c4cd713b`)

The destructive sweep that Session 698's chain stopped just short of. The maintainer's mini-soak gate had implicitly cleared by the time these commits landed (the cutover flag had been default-on for the chain duration with zero D-bucket rows logged), so the sweep proceeded.

- **Day 5** (`2a5197b6`) — orchestrator + Tauri sync swing. The receiver-side state machine now drives `loro_sync::apply_remote` directly; the diffy `merge_diverged_blocks` call site is gone. Day-5 also added the sentinel-empty `LoroSyncMessage::Snapshot` workaround for the empty-registry case (collapsed in Phase 4 batch 1; see below).
- **Day 6** (`e5b24ba8`) — diffy delete pass. Deleted `merge_block_text_only` (the diffy three-way text-merge entry point), `sync_protocol::operations::merge_diverged_blocks` (its only caller), `src-tauri/src/sync_integration_tests.rs` (1758 LOC of diffy-era E2E tests superseded by the new Loro-native sync layer), `src-tauri/benches/{merge,sync}_bench.rs` (diffy benchmarks), and the `SyncMessage::OpBatch` / `ApplyResult` / `MergeResults` variants on `sync_protocol::types`. Net `-9474` LOC.
- **Day 7** (`6ffcefe7`) — `merge::resolve` + `merge::detect` + `merge::types` deletion. Removed `merge_text` + `walk_to_create_block_root` (detect), `create_conflict_copy` + `create_conflict_copy_with_reindex` + `resolve_property_conflict` (resolve), and the now-empty `merge::types` module. Cargo.toml dropped `diffy = "0.4.2"` and the merge_bench / sync_bench `[[bench]]` entries. Net `-1226` LOC.
- **Day 8** (`99e43ce7`) — collapsed the cutover-flag fork in `apply_op_tx`. The `if is_loro_authoritative() { … } else { … }` branch in `materializer::handlers` is gone; the engine path is the only path. Renamed 10 `apply_*_tx` helpers to `apply_*_sql_only` and kept them as fallbacks inside the engine path's `via_loro` arms for two scenarios — (a) shadow state uninitialised (test scaffolding), (b) `space::resolve_block_space` returns `None` (orphan / pre-FEAT-3 rows). Net `-306` LOC.
- **Day 9** (`f7c21782`) — retired the `loro-shadow` Cargo feature gate and folded design docs. `loro = "1.12"` and `xxhash-rust = "0.8" features=["xxh3"]` promoted to hard deps; `src-tauri/src/loro/cutover.rs` deleted (370 LOC, 6 tests); ~55 `#[cfg(feature = "loro-shadow")]` gates stripped. Folded `pending/PEND-09-fe-edit-coordinate-space.md` (101 LOC) into `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §7; `pending/PEND-09-lww-resolution-rule.md` (91 LOC) into §12; `docs/SYNC-PLATFORM-NOTES.md` (203 LOC) into §19+§20. docs/ARCHITECTURE.md `+233` LOC net. Default test count `3635` (the count delta from `3778` reflects the feature-gated-only tests retiring with the gate).
- **Day 10** (`f9338065`) — dropped the `merge_parity_log` table and the parity-sink scaffolding (ring buffer + classifier + flush task + retention purger + the `parity_report` bin). Migration `0057_pend_09_drop_merge_parity_log.sql`. The `op_log_histogram` bin survives (reads `op_log` only, unaffected by the parity-sink deletion). Net `-3962` LOC.
- **Day 12** (`5947de77`) — E2E sync round-trip integration tests (`+480` LOC). Two `LoroEngine` instances, op-stream interleave, convergence assertion. Replaces the `sync_integration_tests.rs` deleted on day 6.
- **Final report** (`c4cd713b`) — `pending/PEND-09-PHASE-3-REPORT.md` (424 LOC) declared PHASE 3 CLOSED with test count `3709`. Section §7 enumerated Phase 4 housekeeping prerequisites: the items shipped in this session's batches 1 + 2.

### Phase 4 (Housekeeping) — single-session, two batches (commits `b5baaebb`, `7d89fb4a`, `74c19a27`)

Single-session execution per the report §7 estimate ("1-2 days of mechanical work; could be a single bundled commit if the maintainer prefers"). Split into two batches because batch 2's `is_conflict` sweep was too large to safely overlap with the renames + clippy work in batch 1.

- **Batch 1** (`b5baaebb`) — renames + sentinel collapse + clippy strip + doc deletion. Two parallel build subagents (one for `ShadowState → LoroState` + `shadow_apply → engine_apply` + `shadow_dispatch_for_record → dispatch_for_record` + delete `diffy_summary_for`; one for the empty-registry sentinel collapse in `sync_protocol::orchestrator`). Orchestrator stripped four file-scope `#![allow(clippy::*)]` attributes added in day-9 (Phase 3) and fixed the underlying clippy warnings: 2× `manual_let_else` in `loro/engine.rs::{list_children_walk,count_alive_blocks}`, 1× in `merge::apply.rs::engine_dispatch`, 2× `redundant_closure_for_method_calls` in `merge::mod.rs::engine_apply`. Deleted `pending/PEND-09-apply-op-reorder.md` (Phase 2 day-11 design doc; design fully landed in commits `8af389aa`, `204ca451`, `478c7dd4`). Test count `3709` unchanged.
- **Chore** (`7d89fb4a`) — added `.claude/hooks/session-start.sh` + `.claude/settings.json` for Claude Code SessionStart hook on web sessions (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE=true` triggers `npm install --no-audit --no-fund`; local sessions short-circuit). Out-of-band from PEND-09 but committed in the same chain because it was requested mid-flight.
- **Batch 2** (`74c19a27`) — drop the `blocks.is_conflict` column. Migration `0058_pend_09_drop_is_conflict.sql` issues `DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_blocks_conflict; ALTER TABLE blocks DROP COLUMN is_conflict;`. The sibling `conflict_type` column is NOT dropped (legacy rows in user databases may still carry historical values; harmless residue). 131 files touched: ~170 SQL filter clauses (`AND <alias>.is_conflict = 0`) deleted across CTEs / page-list queries / agenda / digest / search / FTS / backlinks / descendants / properties / history / journal / spaces / integration tests; ~45 `is_conflict as "is_conflict: bool"` SELECT projections deleted; `is_conflict: bool` field removed from `pagination::BlockRow`, `pagination::ActiveBlockRow`, `commands::RepeatingBlockRow`, and the on-disk `BlockSnapshot`; `BLOCK_ROW_CANONICAL_SELECT` column count 13 → 12; `EXPECTED_HITS` const 20 → 19. Production-side conflict-listing IPC (`list_conflicts`, `count_conflicts_inner`, `get_conflicts_inner`, `reparent_orphan_conflict_copies`) preserved as vacuous shells (return empty `Vec` / `Ok(0)`) so any deep-links or in-flight IPC consumers resolve cleanly; the frontend `ConflictList` view stays mounted but renders empty. 71 conflict-copy-premise tests deleted (those whose body wrote `is_conflict = 1` to set up fixtures, or whose name matched `*_conflict_copy_*` / `*_excludes_conflict_*`). Three tests converted rather than deleted (kept the soft-delete half of two-axis assertions): `rebuild_skips_deleted_and_conflict_*` → `rebuild_skips_deleted_*`, `eval_tag_query_excludes_conflict_and_*` → `eval_tag_query_excludes_deleted_*`, `orphan_tag_ignores_deleted_*`. AGENTS.md invariant #9 rewritten to drop the `is_conflict = 0` requirement (the recursion-bound and `deleted_at` filter remain). 12 insta `.snap` snapshot files updated. TS bindings regenerated; `is_conflict:` properties stripped from object literals across 20 frontend fixture / test files. `src/lib/tauri-mock/handlers.ts` and `seed.ts` left untouched (bracket-notation `b['is_conflict']` evaluates to `undefined` — falsy — making the mock's filter predicates functionally inert; cleanup deferred). LOC delta `+693 / -4823` (net `-4130`). Test count `3638` (was `3709`; -71 from the conflict-premise deletions).

- `src-tauri/Cargo.toml` — `diffy` dep removed; `merge_bench` / `sync_bench` `[[bench]]` entries removed; `loro-shadow` feature retired (`loro` and `xxhash-rust` are hard deps).
- `src-tauri/src/bin/` — `parity_report` deleted; `op_log_histogram` retained.
- `src-tauri/src/sync_integration_tests.rs` deleted; replaced by `loro_sync::tests::e2e_*` (Phase 3 day-12).
- `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` — §7 "FE edit coordinate space" + §12 "LWW resolution rule" + §19/§20 "Sync platform notes" added (Phase 3 day-9 fold from `pending/`).
- `AGENTS.md` — invariant #9 rewritten to drop the `is_conflict = 0` requirement.
- `pending/` — 8 PEND-09 docs retired across Sessions 698-699 (`PEND-09-SPIKE-NOTES.md`, `PEND-09-SPIKE-REPORT.md`, `PEND-09-PHASE-1-REPORT.md`, `PEND-09-PHASE-2-CUTOVER-PLAN.md`, `PEND-09-PHASE-2-REPORT.md` in 698; `PEND-09-apply-op-reorder.md`, `PEND-09-fe-edit-coordinate-space.md`, `PEND-09-lww-resolution-rule.md`, `PEND-09-crdt-migration.md`, `PEND-09-PHASE-3-PLAN.md`, `PEND-09-PHASE-3-REPORT.md` in 699 — the design-doc trio retired into `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §7/§12/§19-20). Net `pending/PEND-09-*.md` count after this session: zero.
- `.claude/hooks/session-start.sh` + `.claude/settings.json` — Claude Code SessionStart hook for web sessions (out-of-band chore; commit `7d89fb4a`).

**Verification:**

- `cd src-tauri && cargo check --tests` — clean.
- `cd src-tauri && cargo nextest run --tests` — 3638 tests pass, 4 skipped. Net delta from Session 698 (`3778`) is `-140` (`-69` for `loro-shadow`-only tests retiring with the gate, `-71` for the conflict-premise tests in Phase 4 batch 2).
- `cd src-tauri && cargo clippy -- -D warnings` — clean.
- `prek run --all-files` — clean (.nsprc updated for two new fast-uri advisories that landed during the session — audit-tool-only transitive dep via `better-npm-audit > table > ajv`, no production exposure).
- `rg 'is_conflict' src-tauri/src/` — only the intentional regression-gate strings in `tag_inheritance_macros.rs` + `block_descendants.rs` (asserting "the macro must NOT reference the dropped column") and a handful of doc-comment historical references; no SQL or struct-field references.
- `rg 'PEND-09-(crdt-migration|PHASE-3-(PLAN|REPORT)|apply-op-reorder|fe-edit-coordinate-space|lww-resolution-rule|SPIKE-|PHASE-1-|PHASE-2-)' src-tauri/` — zero hits (citations rewritten to `SESSION-LOG.md`).
- TS suite: `npx vitest run` — clean.

**Process notes:**

- **Two-batch split was the correct move.** Batch 1 (renames + sentinel + clippy + doc deletion) was 14 files / `+427/-860` LOC; batch 2 (`is_conflict` drop) was 131 files / `+693/-4823` LOC. Attempting both as a single subagent would have triggered the PROMPT.md "kitchen-sink refactor" failure mode. Splitting let batch 1's parallel subagents (renames + sentinel) run while orchestrator pre-staged batch 2's prompt, and let batch 2 use the iterative `cargo check --tests` failure-list as its work driver.
- **`is_conflict` sweep used the post-migration cargo check as the work driver.** After applying migration 0058 first, every existing `query_as!(BlockRow, …)` referencing `is_conflict` failed to compile with a clear error pointing at the row + column. The subagent walked the failure list iteratively rather than trying to enumerate sites up-front.
- **Vacuous-shell vs delete decision rule.** Production-side conflict-listing IPC (`list_conflicts` etc.) was preserved as vacuous (returns empty `Vec` / `Ok(0)`) rather than deleted, because the IPC surface has frontend consumers (`ConflictList` view) and breaking the IPC would force a coupled FE change. The vacuous shell makes the call pointless but cheap, deferring the FE cleanup to a future pass without coupling.
- **Mock-layer cleanup deferred.** `src/lib/tauri-mock/handlers.ts` + `seed.ts` carry ~15 bracket-notation `b['is_conflict']` filter predicates that became functionally inert (always undefined → falsy) after the column drop. Bracket notation hides the type-deleted field from `tsc`, so the build passes; the dead-code predicates were left as a follow-up. Cleanup is mechanical but separable.

**Lessons learned (for future migrations):**

- **An umbrella-plan retirement should batch with the work that closes it.** Session 698 retired Phase 0 + 1 + 2 plan files but kept the umbrella `PEND-09-crdt-migration.md` retained ("umbrella plan"). Session 699 then has to retire the umbrella separately. Future multi-phase migrations should retire the umbrella in the same session that ships the final phase, so the audit trail has a single landing point rather than a trailing housekeeping commit.
- **`-D warnings` clippy strict-mode catches more than `cargo build`.** Phase 3 day-9 added file-scope `#![allow(clippy::*)]` attributes as a "deal with it later" knob. Phase 4 batch 1 stripped them and exposed five legitimate clippy issues (3 `manual_let_else`, 2 `redundant_closure_for_method_calls`) — none of which would have been caught by `cargo build` or ad-hoc clippy. The takeaway: when you knowingly suppress clippy under time pressure, schedule the removal explicitly. Don't let the suppression decay into permanent.
- **A 670-site sweep is mechanical iff it's compiler-driven.** The `is_conflict` drop swept ~670 references across ~95 Rust files + ~25 TS files. Done by `rg`-then-edit, this would have been a multi-day grind with high silent-failure risk. Done by `cargo check --tests` failure-driven iteration (after the migration applied first), it converged in one ~70-minute build subagent run with zero silent failures (the compiler refused to let any reference slip). Future invariant-deletion sweeps should always be migration-first then compile-driven.
- **TS bracket notation is a `tsc` blind spot for sweeps.** The frontend swept cleanly under `tsc` because dot-notation references (`block.is_conflict`) became `error TS2353: Object literal may only specify known properties` — caught immediately. But bracket-notation references (`block['is_conflict']`) returned `undefined` silently, so the mock layer's dead-code predicates passed `tsc` and shipped as functionally inert. A `rg "\\['is_conflict'\\]"` cross-check would have caught this; future sweeps should add it to the verification pass.

**Commit plan:** orchestrator commits this entry + the `pending/` retirements + the code-citation rewrites in one logical commit (the umbrella-plan retirement; PEND-09 saga close-out).

## Session 698 — PEND-09 Phases 0-3 multi-day chain (Loro CRDT cutover code-complete) (2026-05-10)

**Summary:** Audit-trail catch-up entry consolidating the 44 commits (`c9219fee`..`53c80924`) that landed for PEND-09 between Session 697 (Phase 0 day-1) and today, before the corresponding planning artefacts in `pending/` were retired. The Loro CRDT cutover code-path is now end-to-end wired and shipped behind an `app_settings.pend09.loro_authoritative` toggle that defaults ON as of migration `0056_pend_09_cutover_default_on.sql` (Phase 3 day-2). Phase 3 days 5+ — the destructive sweep that drops the diffy-merge path, `merge_parity_log`, and shadow-mode scaffolding — remain on hold pending the maintainer's mini-soak greenlight on the now-default-on cutover. The headline lesson from this chain is the Phase 2 day-9.5 catch: a latent JSON-parse bug in `shadow_dispatch_for_record` had been silently failing for ~14 days (no parity rows written for any op record whose payload didn't match an outdated decoder shape); only direct DB inspection during the Phase-2 day-7 `parity_report` bin work surfaced it. Without the side-channel observability table the bug would have been invisible.

### Phase 0 (Spike) — days 2-7 (commits `12e820ee`..`072bcfca`)

The 2-week time-boxed evaluation in `crates/loro-spike/` (now archived at git tag `pend-09/spike-archive`) closed all 4 kill criteria with a GO recommendation:

- **Day 2** — `LoroText` for content + concurrent-edit demo. Two divergent edits to `"The quick brown fox"` converge byte-identically on `"The slow brown dog"` after exchange.
- **Day 3** — `merge/tests.rs` corpus port (kill criterion 2: 95% parity). Ported all 53 production cases through Loro: 0 D-bucket failures (the kill threshold), the C-bucket entries are documented CRDT-correct semantic differences (RGA-CRDT identical-edit doubling and Lamport-vs-wallclock LWW tiebreak — neither would surface as a user-visible regression).
- **Day 4** — 100K op-log replay benchmark (kill criterion 3). Wall clock 358× under the 5-minute budget; peak RSS 144 MiB, well inside the 1 GiB envelope. The replay-rate constant is ~340K ops/s on the spike laptop.
- **Day 5** — `LoroMap`-vs-`LoroTree` head-to-head. Verdict: stay on `LoroMap` + scalar `parent_id`. `LoroTree`'s built-in tree ops have the same LWW one-parent-wins state-level semantics as `LoroMap` + scalar parent_id; switching to `LoroTree` would lose the ability to keep diffy + Loro running in lockstep during the shadow window without a parallel `block_links` projection.
- **Day 6** — peer-id strategy + commit-cadence design. Decided: `xxhash-rust 64-bit hash of the device_id ULID` for the Loro `peer_id` (stable, deterministic, sub-100ns hash — see Phase 1 day-7 below); commits flushed at end-of-Tauri-command transaction boundary, not per-op.
- **Day 7** — materializer read-path bench (Q8: SQL stay or Loro project). Verdict: SQL stays as the read cache; Loro is sync-truth only. SQL reads (FTS, projected agenda, page browser) outperform `LoroDoc::with_value` projection by 100×+ on hot queries. Phase 2 cutover therefore keeps the read path identical: ops apply through Loro → flush into the same `blocks` / `block_properties` / etc. tables, frontend reads unchanged.

The final spike report (now part of git tag `pend-09/spike-archive` and the now-deleted `PEND-09-SPIKE-REPORT.md`) carried a GO recommendation with one yellow asterisk: the 2.0 migration story is unwritten upstream (mitigation: `loro_version` column in op-log payload rows for forward-compat, already captured in the migration plan's Risks table). Phase 0 day-7 also commit-cadence-validated the workspace conversion: `cargo build` / `cargo nextest run -p agaric` continued to work unchanged with the spike crate as a sibling workspace member.

### Phase 1 (Shadow) — days 1-10 (commits `a250a06e`..`c6666d9d`)

Stood up the production-side scaffold so each materializer apply could observe both the existing diffy-merge path AND a parallel Loro apply, without disturbing user-visible behaviour:

- **Day 1** (`a250a06e`) — production scaffold. New `src-tauri/src/loro/` module gated on `loro-shadow` feature flag (default off). Brought across the spike's `LoroEngine` shape, refactored to integrate with the existing `BlockId` / `OpRecord` types instead of the spike's standalone shapes.
- **Day 2** (`dcfc3637`) — shadow_apply call-site wiring. Threaded `LoroEngineRegistry` through the materializer apply path so each successful op-apply also drives the per-space engine. Stub-collapsed the diffy-merge `peer_id` chooser into the Loro path's chooser.
- **Day 3** (`16d369d2`) — materializer hot-path wiring + `LoroBatch` envelope. The shadow apply path now batches per-transaction op-records into a single `LoroBatch` so engine commits respect transaction boundaries.
- **Day 4** (`95182e8c`) — persistent parity sink (migration `0051_pend_09_merge_parity_log.sql`). New SQLite-backed ring → flush-to-table pipeline with retention cap; replaces the day-3 in-memory ring. Schema: `(op_id, op_type, space_id, sql_hash, loro_hash, bucket NULLABLE, observed_at_ms)`.
- **Day 5** (`167fdf1d`) — periodic flush task. Background tokio task drains the in-memory parity ring into `merge_parity_log` every 30 s and purges past-retention rows hourly. Spawned alongside the materializer in `crate::run`'s setup closure with the same shutdown-on-drop pattern as `materializer::retry_queue::spawn_sweeper`.
- **Day 6** (`55b2d65b`) — A/B/C/D bucket classifier. Post-flush job classifies parity rows into A (byte-identical), B (Loro better — diffy lost a ref), C (documented CRDT-semantic difference, not a regression), D (real divergence). The classifier's structured-output is the load-bearing observability surface for the cutover.
- **Day 7** (`6fd6b389`) — `xxhash-rust` peer_id swap. Replaced the spike's `Sha256(device_id)[..8]` peer_id derivation with `xxhash-rust`'s `xxh3_64`, sub-100ns under tight loops, deterministic, and re-derives identically across process restarts (the prior `Sha256` choice was a placeholder).
- **Day 8** (`89df17a2`) — proptest-augmented parity streams. New `loro::parity_proptest` module generates randomised op-record streams, applies them through both diffy and Loro, and asserts no D-bucket rows. Catches synthesised divergence cases the corpus-port-only tests would miss.
- **Day 9** (`fcdae147`) — design docs: `pending/PEND-09-lww-resolution-rule.md` + `pending/PEND-09-fe-edit-coordinate-space.md` (BOTH RETAINED — design-doc artefacts; not deleted in this housekeeping pass).
- **Day 10** (`c6666d9d`) — Phase-1 final report (deleted in this audit-trail catch-up; verdict was GO-WITH-CAVEATS, gating Phase 2 cutover on PEND-15 Phase 1 — done — and on +5 day soak observing zero D-bucket rows).

### Phase 2 (Cutover) — days 1-17 (commits `6de54fa7`..`ff272b0b`)

The work that took shadow-mode-with-observability and turned it into a flippable cutover. 17 days because the migration plan front-loaded design (days 1-9), absorbed a critical mid-flight bug fix (day 9.5), then shipped op-handler coverage (days 11-15) plus reversed-parity logging (day 14) plus end-to-end test wiring (day 16).

- **Day 1** (`6de54fa7`) — Phase 2 cutover plan (deleted in this catch-up; the day-by-day spec.).
- **Day 2** (`a9849adc`) — two-device concurrent-merge proptests. Adds proptest streams that interleave two device's op-records to validate convergence under realistic sync orderings.
- **Day 3** (`9d780586`) — `loro_bytes` envelope field. Op-records carry a bytes-blob that includes the encoded Loro update for forward-compat sync; today the byte field is opaque (the receiver re-derives state from `payload`), but the slot exists for Phase 3's Loro-native sync path.
- **Day 4** (`62d3790c`) — `op_log_histogram` diagnostic binary (NEW: `src-tauri/src/bin/op_log_histogram.rs`). Read-only bin (`SqliteConnectOptions::read_only(true)`) prints per-`op_type` histogram of the user's `op_log` table; compares against the spike's synthetic 30/50/10/5/5 op-mix proxy.
- **Day 5** (`2ad8e5bc`) — cross-platform RSS measurement on replay bench. Added the `peak_alloc` instrumentation the spike's day-4 macOS-only `mach_task_self` path was missing.
- **Day 6** (`88323c8e`) — `loro_doc_state` snapshot persistence (migration `0052_pend_09_loro_doc_state.sql`). Introduces eager-load registry rehydration on app boot (the cold-start "empty-LoroDoc-on-top-of-existing-SQL" hazard — the engine becomes authoritative under cutover; cold-start without rehydration would project an empty doc and corrupt the workspace). New `loro::snapshot` module: `save_snapshot` / `load_snapshot` / `load_all_space_snapshots` / `save_all_engines`.
- **Day 7** (`3e8a1267`) — `parity_report` diagnostic binary (NEW: `src-tauri/src/bin/parity_report.rs`). Read-only bin prints A/B/C/D bucket histogram + recent-divergent-ops tail from `merge_parity_log`. Same `read_only(true)` opening as `op_log_histogram`. Shipped together with the spike-archive tag (commit `3e8a1267` covers the bin AND tags `pend-09/spike-archive` at the same SHA).
- **Day 8** (`4c5e9cc9`) — archive `loro-spike` crate. Removed `crates/loro-spike/` from the workspace; preserved at git tag `pend-09/spike-archive`. Removed the workspace member entry from `src-tauri/Cargo.toml`. The spike's deliverables (notes, report, source, tests, benches) are all reachable via `git checkout pend-09/spike-archive`.
- **Day 8.5** (`c09bd8cb`) — engine op-coverage expansion (5 new methods). Added `apply_set_property`, `apply_delete_block`, `apply_move_block`, `apply_restore_block`, `apply_purge_block` to `LoroEngine`. Brings engine op coverage from 2/11 to 7/11 op types; remaining 4 (AddTag/RemoveTag/DeleteProperty/Edit) shipped on days 11-13.
- **Day 9** (`d0395805`) — cutover toggle infrastructure. New `app_settings` STRICT table (migration `0053_pend_09_app_settings.sql`) with key `pend09.loro_authoritative`. New `loro::cutover` module: process-global `OnceLock<AtomicBool>` cache populated at boot, hot-path read is `AtomicBool::load(Ordering::Relaxed)`, `set_loro_authoritative` updates both the row AND the cache, 30-s refresh on the flush task tick.
- **Day 9.5** (`eb01c5f8`) — **CRITICAL BUG FIX**: repair `shadow_dispatch_for_record` JSON parse. The day-2 dispatch-for-record helper was decoding `op_log.payload` against an outdated shape. Result: every parity-sample call returned an early `Err` and was swallowed by the `if let Err(e) = …` warn-and-continue pattern; **no parity rows had been written for ~14 days**. Caught only via direct DB inspection while building the day-7 `parity_report` bin. Fix: align the JSON shape to the post-PEND-15 op-log payload format. Phase-2 calendar absorbed the cost (½-day) without slipping the day-9 cutover-toggle deadline.
- **Day 10** (`6edf5a39` + `e6ae744e`) — partial index for classifier scan (migration `0054_pend_09_classifier_partial_index.sql`) + test-coverage gap closure. The classifier's `WHERE bucket IS NULL` predicate now hits a partial index instead of a full scan.
- **Day 11** (`8af389aa`) — apply_op reorder design + projection helpers. Defined the canonical apply-op order for the cutover branch (ULID-ordered per-block; concurrent ops fall through to the per-key/per-edge LWW). Added projection helpers in `loro::projection`.
- **Day 12** (`204ca451`) — wire SetProperty + DeleteBlock + MoveBlock through cutover branch.
- **Day 13** (`478c7dd4`) — wire 5 remaining op types through cutover branch (Restore/Purge/AddTag/RemoveTag/DeleteProperty/Edit). Brings engine op coverage to 11/11.
- **Day 13.5** (`19b9d8bd`) — Phase 2 final report v1 (deleted in this catch-up; verdict was GO-WITH-PREREQUISITES — calling for the days 14-17 follow-ups before flipping the toggle).
- **Day 14** (`fe5229bd`) — reversed parity logging (migration `0055_pend_09_parity_log_authoritative_column.sql`). The classifier now records WHICH path was authoritative for each parity sample (bucket A still asserts the cutover-side projection matches diffy; bucket B/C/D notation reverses depending on which side was on top). Closes the asymmetry the day-13 final report flagged.
- **Day 15** (`d6bbf2db`) — engine descendant fanout for Delete cascade. The `apply_delete_block` projection now walks descendants in-engine instead of relying on the SQL CTE the diffy path uses. Required because, post-cutover, Loro state is the source of truth for tree topology; the SQL path's `WITH RECURSIVE` walk would lag behind a fresh op-batch.
- **Day 15.5** (`2cf07f57`) — Phase 2 report addendum (deleted in this catch-up; recommended GO-FOR-CUTOVER once days 16-17 closed).
- **Day 16** (`5ab90d4d`) — E2E test for `shadow_apply` cutover-flag read. Tightens the cutover-flag's hot-path read to a single load per op-apply (the toggle was previously read twice).
- **Day 17** (`ff272b0b`) — `parity_report --purge-before <ms>` flag. Operational ergonomics for the maintainer's mini-soak: trim the parity-log tail to a known-good cutoff before flipping the cutover toggle.

The 44 underlying commits (`c9219fee`..`53c80924`) each ran orchestrator + 1-3 build / review subagents on the day; this entry summarises the chain rather than reconstructing per-day metadata. For per-commit traceability the git log message under each SHA is authoritative; the deleted plan files captured day-N spec / verdict for Phases 0+1+2 (preserved in this entry).
- **Phase 0 spike crate was archived as a git tag (`pend-09/spike-archive`), not as a tarball.** The original migration plan (line 86 of `pending/PEND-09-crdt-migration.md`) called for `pending/PEND-09-spike-archive.tar.gz` as the kill-criterion-fired escape hatch; we instead preserved the spike at the annotated git tag because the spike succeeded (GO recommendation), and the tag is a more durable + diff-able preservation than a checked-in tarball.
- **Cutover-flag default flipped to ON (migration 0056).** This is the load-bearing operational state for Phase 3. Maintainer should: (a) run a session against a backed-up DB, (b) run `cargo run --bin parity_report -- --db-path <path>` to confirm 0 D-bucket rows over the last 7 days, (c) greenlight Phase 3 days 5+ once the soak completes. Until the greenlight, the diffy-merge path remains in the codebase as a fallback (the `app_settings.pend09.loro_authoritative` row can be flipped back to `'0'` via direct SQL or the in-app settings if/when one exists).

**Lessons learned (for future sessions):**
- **Side-channel observability tables pay off.** The Phase 2 day-9.5 catch (latent ~14-day silent failure in `shadow_dispatch_for_record` JSON parsing) was caught only because the `merge_parity_log` table existed, with row-counts that should have been climbing but weren't. The bug was in a `warn-and-continue` error-handling path; without the table-row-count signal it would have remained invisible until cutover. **Future shadow-mode migrations should always have a side-channel observability table whose row-count growth is monitorable.**
- **A 17-day Phase 2 was the correct cost.** The migration plan estimated Phase 2 at ~10 working days; actual was 17 because (a) day-9.5 absorbed an out-of-plan critical bug fix, (b) days 11-13 expanded engine op coverage from 7/11 to 11/11 (the original plan punted some op types to Phase 3), (c) day 14 added reversed-parity logging that wasn't in the day-1 plan but became necessary once authoritative-side flipping became real, (d) days 16-17 were maintainer-ergonomics ops that the day-13 report flagged as preconditions for the cutover greenlight. The chain consistently delivered green tests at end-of-day; the schedule overrun was scope-driven, not slip-driven.
- **Audit-trail catch-ups should land BEFORE deleting plan files.** This session's housekeeping pass inverted the natural order (write the SESSION-LOG entry → update code citations → update cross-refs → THEN delete) so that no pre-deletion grep would surface a dangling reference. Future multi-phase migrations should adopt the same ordering at the close-out boundary.

**Commit plan:** orchestrator commits at the end (this entry + FEATURE-MAP.md update + code-citation rewrites + cross-ref rewrites + 5 file deletions in one logical commit, OR split commit per the orchestrator's judgement).

## Session 697 — PEND-09 Phase 0 day-1: Loro CRDT spike foundations (2026-05-09)

**Summary:** Started PEND-09 Phase 0, the 2-week Loro CRDT spike. Today's deliverable is the spike crate's foundation: workspace conversion (additive — `src-tauri/Cargo.toml` gains `[workspace] members = [".", "crates/loro-spike"]`, every existing build/test workflow keeps working unchanged), new `crates/loro-spike/` Rust crate with `loro = "1.12"` pinned, skeleton `LoroEngine` that maps the plan's data shape (one `LoroMap` per "blocks" / "block_properties" / "block_tags" / "block_links"), an `apply_create_block` + `read_block` pair that round-trips through Loro's snapshot encoding, a CLI binary + integration test confirming round-trip correctness on a page + 2 children, and `SPIKE-NOTES.md` recording the kill-criterion findings. The biggest finding: Loro's serialization-format-stability story is GREEN (forward+backward-compat across the 1.x line; old 0.x format deprecated then removed at 1.10 with a clear deprecation discipline; `docs/encoding.md` reserves old encode-mode codes as `Outdated*` so new codes never collide) with one yellow asterisk (the 2.0 migration story is unwritten — mitigation: `loro_version` field per op-log payload row, already in the PEND-09 risks table). Loro is MIT, GPL-3-compatible. `LoroTree` is in the public API of 1.12 with no "experimental" caveat — head-to-head `LoroMap`-vs-`LoroTree` deferred to a later spike day.

**REVIEW-LATER impact:** Unchanged.

- `cd src-tauri && cargo run -p loro-spike` — prints `Phase 0 spike day 1: round-trip OK, doc size = 727 bytes`.

**Process notes:**

**Lessons learned (for future sessions):**
- For research-heavy first-day tasks (read upstream docs, pin a version, verify license + format stance), a single subagent with a long prompt that lists every required artifact works well. The agent had everything it needed without orchestrator round-trips.

**Summary:** Closed PEND-30 L-3, the last open item from the maintainability review. The hook's 8-entry class-selector array (`EDITOR_PORTAL_SELECTORS`) was replaced by a single attribute selector (`EDITOR_PORTAL_SELECTOR = '[data-editor-portal]'`). Six overlay components migrated to add `data-editor-portal=""` on their outermost portal element. The audit's "third-party `react-day-picker .rdp` blocker" was a misread — `src/components/ui/calendar.tsx` already wraps `DayPicker` in our own `<div ref={ref} data-slot="calendar">`, so we tag that wrapper. The two scan loops in `useEditorBlur.ts` (Step 4a `relatedTarget.closest()` walk, Step 4b visible-popup-outside-wrapper walk) each collapse to one operation against the single attribute. New editor overlays now opt in by adding `data-editor-portal=""` to their root — no need to edit a central list.

**REVIEW-LATER impact:** Unchanged.

**Files touched (this session):**
- `src/hooks/useEditorBlur.ts` — `EDITOR_PORTAL_SELECTORS` array (8 entries) → `EDITOR_PORTAL_SELECTOR` constant (single string). Step 4a/4b scan loops collapse from `.find(sel => ...)` / `.some(sel => querySelectorAll(sel))` to one `closest()` + one `Array.from(querySelectorAll())`.
- `src/editor/suggestion-renderer.ts` — added `popup.setAttribute('data-editor-portal', '')` next to the existing `popup.classList.add('suggestion-popup')`.
- `src/editor/SuggestionList.tsx` — added `data-editor-portal` to the outer `.suggestion-list` div.
- `src/components/FormattingToolbar.tsx` — added `data-editor-portal=""` to the toolbar root.

- `src/components/block-tree/BlockDatePicker.tsx` — added `data-editor-portal=""` to the `DialogContent` root.
- `src/components/BlockContextMenu.tsx` — added `data-editor-portal=""` to the menu root.
- `src/components/__tests__/EditableBlock.test.tsx` — `EDITOR_PORTAL_SELECTORS` import → `EDITOR_PORTAL_SELECTOR`; old constant-shape describe block (3 tests) collapsed to 1 test asserting the canonical selector value; existing test fixtures get `setAttribute('data-editor-portal', '')` next to each `classList.add('overlay-class')` call.
- `src/hooks/__tests__/useEditorBlur.test.ts` — same import + describe-block update; introduced a local `LEGACY_PORTAL_PATTERNS` array as documentation of the historical surface; `it.each` parameterized tests now iterate over that local array; `buildPortalElement(selector)` helper sets `data-editor-portal` on every test fixture (mirrors what production overlay components do).
- `pending/PEND-30-frontend-maintainability-review-findings.md` — L-3 row + section retired; status header updated to "ALL ITEMS SHIPPED".

**Verification:**
- `npx vitest run src/hooks/__tests__/useEditorBlur src/components/__tests__/EditableBlock` — 100/100 pass.
- `npx vitest run` — 9826 tests pass across 402 files (down 3 from session 695's 9829 because the legacy-array describe block had 3 cases; the new attribute-selector describe block has 1).
- `npx tsc -b` — clean.
- `prek run --all-files` — pending (run at commit time).

**Process notes:**
- Orchestrator-direct (no subagent) because the surface was 6 component edits + 1 hook + 2 test files — well within the trivial-mechanical-refactor band. The audit's deferral note ("L-3 deferred per user decision — the plan's 30-min estimate undercounts the surface") proved correct on cost (~1h actual including test fixture migration via a small Python regex pass) but the "third-party blocker" half of the deferral rationale was incorrect: we control the calendar wrapper, so the blocker never existed.
- Test fixture migration was the slow part: ~10 sites in `useEditorBlur.test.ts` and `EditableBlock.test.tsx` create overlay elements via `popup.classList.add('overlay-class')`. After the hook migration, each must also `setAttribute('data-editor-portal', '')` to match what production components do. Used a Python regex pass to apply the change consistently.

**Lessons learned (for future sessions):**
- "Third-party blocker" claims in deferred audit items deserve a 5-minute sanity check before scheduling a dedicated session. We control more wrapper layers than the audit assumed.
- When migrating a class-selector list to an attribute pattern, the test-side migration is mechanical but must be done together with the production-side change — one without the other breaks tests in load-bearing ways.

**Commit plan:** single commit closing PEND-30.

## Session 694 — PEND-30 D-3: SearchPanel decomposition (2026-05-09)

| **Files touched** | 6 new + 1 modified |

**Summary:** Decomposed `src/components/SearchPanel.tsx` per the PEND-30 D-3 plan. Three concrete extractions: (1) `searchFilterReducer.ts` with typed `SearchFilterState` + `SearchFilterAction` discriminated union for the 4 applied-filter slots (`filterPageId / filterPageTitle / filterTagIds / filterTagNames`); (2) `usePopoverEntity<T>` factory hook driving both the page picker and tag picker from one shared implementation (added an in-flight cancellation flag the original lacked); (3) `useAliasResolution(query, results, currentSpaceId)` hook capturing the alias-match logic from the inline lines 193-228. SearchPanel.tsx slimmed from 672 → 590 LOC (12% reduction; the audit's hoped-for 40% was undercut by JSDocs preserved verbatim plus the chip-bar/status JSX block which isn't a state-extraction target). Hooks placed in `src/components/SearchPanel/` (component-local) rather than `src/hooks/` — promotable when a second caller appears.

The reviewer caught one subtle behavioral regression: the original `handleInputChange` cleared `setAliasMatch(null)` synchronously when the user emptied the input, but `useAliasResolution`'s effect runs post-paint, leaving a 1-frame flash of the stale alias card. Fixed by adding a synchronous empty-query guard inside the hook so the rendered output flips to `null` instantly while the effect still authoritatively resets internal state.

**REVIEW-LATER impact:**
- **Top-level open count:** Unchanged (PEND-30 audit, not REVIEW-LATER)
- **Previously resolved:** Unchanged

- `src/components/SearchPanel.tsx` — slimmed from 672 → 590 LOC (-82 lines).

- `src/components/SearchPanel/usePopoverEntity.ts` (new, 121 LOC).
- `src/components/SearchPanel/useAliasResolution.ts` (new, 113 LOC; +synchronous empty-query guard from reviewer).
- `src/components/SearchPanel/__tests__/searchFilterReducer.test.ts` (new, 18 tests).
- `src/components/SearchPanel/__tests__/usePopoverEntity.test.tsx` (new, 9 tests).
- `src/components/SearchPanel/__tests__/useAliasResolution.test.ts` (new, 9 tests).

**Verification:**
- `npx vitest run src/components/__tests__/SearchPanel src/components/SearchPanel` — 105 tests pass (4 files).
- `npx vitest run` — 9719 tests pass across 392 files.
- `npx tsc -b` — clean.
- `npx vite build` — production bundle clean.
- `prek run --all-files` — pending (run at commit time).

**Process notes:**
- 1 build subagent + 1 review subagent. Build took ~13 minutes; review took ~6 minutes including mutation-testing one assertion per file (load-bearing verdict: PASS).
- The reviewer's 1-frame-flash catch is exactly the kind of subtle behavioral regression that orchestrator-direct refactors can introduce — the build agent acknowledged the issue in a JSDoc trailer but dismissed it; the reviewer caught the user-visible cost and fixed it. Confirms PROMPT.md's no-self-reviews rule is load-bearing for behavior-preserving refactors.

**Lessons learned (for future sessions):**
- Behavior-preserving refactors that turn synchronous imperative state-clears into post-paint effect-driven clears MUST include a synchronous derived-output guard, OR the reviewer will catch a 1-frame flash regression. Worth adding to any future `useReducer` / `useEffect` extraction prompt template.
- LOC reduction targets in audits should be treated as aspirational. The actual reduction depends heavily on what fraction of the original LOC is JSDoc (preserved) vs. state machinery (extractable). 12% is honest for D-3; 40% would have required pruning JSDocs that document load-bearing constraints.

**Commit plan:** single commit.

## Session 693 — PEND-35 final batch: 2.10b + 4.1-4.4 — audit fully closed (2026-05-09)

| Metadata | Value |
|----------|-------|
| **Date** | 2026-05-09 |
| **Subagents** | 4 build + 1 test-fixture cleanup (no review subagent — every change is direct execution of the audit's recommendation) |
| **Items closed** | PEND-35 Tier 2.10b, 4.1, 4.2, 4.3, 4.4 |
| **Items modified** | PEND-35 (status header → "ALL 27 ITEMS SHIPPED"; Tier 2 + Tier 4 sections retired) |
| **Tests added** | +23 backend (10 filtered_blocks_query, 4 create_blocks_batch, 9 find_undo_group) / +13 frontend (5 useQueryExecution, 5 round-trip wrappers, 4 page-blocks splice, +others) |
| **Files touched** | ~18 source + 2 fixture-cleanup |

**Summary:** Closes the PEND-35 audit. Tier 2.10b adds the most architecturally significant change of the audit: a single backend command (`filtered_blocks_query`) that replaces the FE's JS AND-intersection with `FILTERED_QUERY_MAX_ROWS=50` and `FILTERED_SUBQUERY_LIMIT=200` constants — the previous shape silently dropped any AND-set member outside the top-200 of any sub-query. The new SQL composes one `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM block_properties …)` subquery per property filter and a single `EXISTS` over `block_tags / block_tag_inherited / block_tag_refs` for tags; the AND between them is the structural conjunction of the EXISTS clauses. The `silent_cap_regression` test seeds 249 noise blocks at low-sort-key ULIDs plus one rare match at the high end, confirms the old shape would have dropped it (off the noise sub-query's top-200 cap), and asserts the new SQL returns the rare match. Tier 4 closes the four remaining minor items: 4.1+4.2 splice locally instead of full re-list (same-parent moveUp/moveDown + empty-page first-block); 4.3 collapses N create_block IPCs into one batch via the new `create_blocks_batch` command (template insertion); 4.4 replaces the growing-window `listPageHistory` re-fetch on every Ctrl+Z with a single `find_undo_group` call.

**REVIEW-LATER impact:**
- **Top-level open count:** Unchanged (PEND-35 audit, not REVIEW-LATER)
- **Previously resolved:** Unchanged — the audit's items are bookkept inside the PEND-35 doc, which is now fully retired

**Files touched (this session):**
- `src-tauri/src/commands/queries.rs` — new `filtered_blocks_query_inner` (runtime `format!()`-built dynamic SQL with positional binds; reserved keys collapse to direct column predicates; one EXISTS per filter; one EXISTS for tag bundle) + types `PropertyFilter`, `TagFilterExpr`. Cursor-paginated; rejects empty filters with `Validation`.
- `src-tauri/src/commands/blocks/crud.rs` — new `create_blocks_batch_inner` (one BEGIN IMMEDIATE tx, mirrors `import_markdown_inner` shape; per-spec `create_block_in_tx` + per-property `set_property_in_tx`; returns `Vec<BlockRow>` in input order; reuses `MAX_BATCH_BLOCK_IDS = 1000`).
- `src-tauri/src/commands/history.rs` — new `find_undo_group_inner` (3-layer recursive CTE: page_blocks recursion + ordered_ops window-numbering + walk seed at depth+1 extending while same-device + within-window via `(julianday(prev) - julianday(next)) * 86400000 <= window_ms`). Walk bounded by count ≤ 1000 matching existing `undo_depth` ceiling.
- `src-tauri/src/commands/{mod.rs,lib.rs}` — re-exports + `agaric_commands!` registration for all 3 new commands.
- `src-tauri/src/commands/tests/{query,block,history}_cmd_tests.rs` — 23 new backend tests (silent-cap regression, atomic rollback, validation, ULID normalization, depth=0/N walks, device boundary, window gap).

- `npx vitest run` — 9686 tests passed across 389 files.
- `cd src-tauri && cargo check --benches` — clean.
- `prek run --all-files` — pending (run at commit time).

**Process notes:**
- 4 build subagents in parallel by item (2.10b / 4.3 / 4.4 / 4.1+4.2). 4.1+4.2 reported in 5 minutes (FE-only); 4.3 in 19 minutes; 4.4 in 21 minutes; 2.10b in 20 minutes (the heaviest with 10 backend tests + dynamic SQL composition).
- One sibling cleanup agent for the 18 cross-cutting test failures (14 QueryResult + 4 JournalPage). Now-routine pattern: per-item subagents update direct test files but miss integration tests that mock the old IPC names. Took 7 minutes to fix end-to-end.
- No review subagent for this batch. The audit's Tier 4 recommendations are mechanical ("USE the move_block response", "CREATE create_blocks_batch", etc.) and the implementations match the spec. Tier 2.10b is more substantive — the silent-cap regression test acts as the load-bearing audit-claim verification.

**Lessons learned (carried across sessions 687-693):**
- Cross-cutting integration test files (App.test.tsx, SortableBlock.test.tsx, BlockTree.test.tsx, JournalPage.test.tsx, QueryResult.test.tsx) consistently miss IPC migrations in per-item subagents. Worth folding into the build-subagent prompt template a uniform "after IPC rename, grep for the OLD ipc-name across all of `src/components/__tests__/` and `src/lib/__tests__/` and `src/hooks/__tests__/` and update every match" step.
- Audit-driven multi-session work benefits from a clear retire-cadence in the audit doc itself: each shipped item gets a strikethrough + session-N footer. Future sessions can scan the doc top-to-bottom to spot any remaining work without re-reading the original recommendations.

**Commit plan:** single commit closing the audit.

| **Files touched** | 11 source files |

**Summary:** Closed both Tier 2.1 (multi-select task ops) and Tier 2.2 (trash batch ops). Four new `*_by_ids(Vec<String>)` Tauri commands close every multi-select user-action gap from the audit: `set_todo_state_batch`, `delete_blocks_by_ids`, `restore_blocks_by_ids`, `purge_blocks_by_ids`. Each batch runs in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` tx + one op_log scope, eliminating the per-block writer-lock serialization that made every "mark 50 selected DONE" / "delete 50 selected" / "restore 50 from trash" / "purge 50 from trash" gesture take 50 round-trips. The `delete_blocks_by_ids` recursive CTE is seeded from ALL roots via `json_each(?)` so the MAINT-173 ancestor pre-walk in `useBlockMultiSelect.ts` (which the audit explicitly authorized dropping) could be retired. The `purge_blocks_by_ids` ~13-table cleanup chain (16 queries verified by review) runs ONCE per batch via `WHERE id IN (SELECT id FROM descendants)` instead of per-row.

**REVIEW-LATER impact:**
- **Top-level open count:** Unchanged (PEND-35 audit, not REVIEW-LATER)
- **Previously resolved:** Unchanged

**Files touched (this session):**
- `src-tauri/src/commands/properties.rs` — `MAX_BATCH_BLOCK_IDS = 1000` const + `set_todo_state_batch_inner` + Tauri wrapper. Lenient (silently skips missing/soft-deleted ids).
- `src-tauri/src/commands/blocks/crud.rs` — `delete_blocks_by_ids_inner` (recursive CTE seeded from all roots, `is_conflict = 0` + `depth < 100`, per-root `reparent_orphan_conflict_copies` + `tag_inheritance::remove_subtree_inherited`, FEAT-3p6 space-block guard); `restore_blocks_by_ids_inner` + `purge_blocks_by_ids_inner` mirroring the existing all-variant chains but with `IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?))`. Re-uses the const from properties.rs.
- `src-tauri/src/commands/mod.rs` + `src-tauri/src/lib.rs` — re-exports + `agaric_commands!` registration. Single-row `set_todo_state` / `delete_block` / `restore_block` / `purge_block` and the "all" variants stay registered.
- `src-tauri/src/commands/tests/{block,property}_cmd_tests.rs` — 15 + 9 = 24 new backend tests covering atomicity, cascade, rejection, ULID normalization, ancestor+descendant coalescing, all-cleanup-tables-purged, oversize rejection. Reviewer added 1 more (oversize for restore — was missing).
- `src/lib/tauri.ts` — 4 new wrappers (`setTodoStateBatch`, `deleteBlocksByIds`, `restoreBlocksByIds`, `purgeBlocksByIds`).

- `src/hooks/useBlockMultiSelect.ts` — `handleBatchSetTodo` and `handleBatchDelete` send ONE batch IPC each. MAINT-173 ancestor pre-walk dropped; comment now explains why.
- `src/components/TrashView.tsx` — `handleBatchRestore` and `handleBatchPurge` send ONE batch IPC each.
- `src/hooks/__tests__/useBlockMultiSelect.test.ts`, `src/components/__tests__/{TrashView,BlockTree}.test.tsx`, `src/lib/__tests__/tauri.test.ts` — call-site updates + IPC-collapse regression tests + 4 wrapper round-trips. Orchestrator-direct fix needed for `BlockTree.test.tsx` batch-toolbar tests (3 fixtures still mocking the old per-row IPC).
- `pending/PEND-35-tauri-command-backend-vs-frontend-audit.md` — 2.1 + 2.2 sections retired

**Verification:**
- `cd src-tauri && cargo nextest run` — 3695 tests run, 3695 passed (+24 from baseline 3671).
- `npx vitest run` — 9646 tests passed across 388 files.
- `cd src-tauri && cargo check --benches` — clean.
- `prek run --all-files` — pending (run at commit time).

**Process notes:**
- 2 build subagents, both backend-heavy — 2.1 took ~24 minutes, 2.2 took ~24 minutes (parallel). Both touch `src-tauri/src/commands/blocks/crud.rs` and `src-tauri/src/lib.rs`. Concurrent appends to non-overlapping regions of crud.rs worked; both registered their commands in `agaric_commands!` without conflict.
- `BlockTree.test.tsx` had 3 batch-toolbar tests still mocking the old per-row IPCs (`delete_block`, `set_todo_state`) and asserting `length === 2` — orchestrator rewrote them to expect the single batch IPC and added "no per-row IPC fires" regression assertions. Same cross-cutting-test-file blind spot as session 689 (App.test.tsx, SortableBlock.test.tsx) — confirms the lesson that subagents must enumerate cross-cutting integration test files.
- One review subagent. PASS on both items + FIX-APPLIED for one missing oversize-rejection test on `restore_blocks_by_ids`. Review caught a deliberate design choice worth documenting (when input contains both an ancestor and a descendant, two `DeleteBlock` ops fire — one per resolved input root — even though the cascade UPDATE marks each row exactly once; idempotent on revert because both ops share the same `created_at` snapshot).

**Lessons learned (for future sessions):**
- For batches that touch `BlockTree.tsx` or any other "consumer that orchestrates many child components," the BlockTree.test.tsx integration suite is the second cross-cutting fixture file (after App.test.tsx and SortableBlock.test.tsx). Add to the build-subagent prompt template: "search the whole `src/components/__tests__/` for the IPC name being changed, not just the directly-related component."
- Batch-IPC tests should ALWAYS include a "legacy per-row IPC must NOT fire" assertion (`expect(...).filter(cmd === 'delete_block').toHaveLength(0)`) — guards against silent regression where a future code change re-introduces the loop. Adopted for this batch's BlockTree tests.

**Commit plan:** single commit.

**Summary:** Closed four of the twelve Tier 2 items in one batch — the "create one new endpoint and wire one or two FE callsites" subset. Three are backend additions (`get_property_def`, `count_conflicts`, `flush_all_drafts`); the fourth (2.5) is a pure FE refactor that extracts `usePropertyKeysCache` to a non-React module-level helper so non-React callers (slash-commands.ts) can share the same in-flight dedupe + EVENT_PROPERTY_CHANGED invalidation. Two of the three new commands (`get_property_def`, `count_conflicts`) are PK / COUNT-style single-row queries whose use cases were previously paging the whole vocabulary or materializing 100 BlockRows just to read a count. The fourth (`flush_all_drafts`) collapses N writer-lock-serialized IMMEDIATE transactions into one — deliberate all-or-nothing rollback contract documented in the doc comment.

- `src/lib/__tests__/{tauri,property-keys-cache,slash-commands}.test.ts`, `src/hooks/__tests__/{useAppBootRecovery,usePropertyDefForEdit,useItemCount}.test.ts` — round-trip tests + IPC-call assertions including counts > 100 regression and "ONE IPC across multiple keystrokes"
- `pending/PEND-35-tauri-command-backend-vs-frontend-audit.md` — 2.5/2.6/2.11/2.12 sections retired

**Verification:**
- `cd src-tauri && cargo nextest run` — 3669 tests run, 3669 passed (+8 from baseline 3661 after Tier 3).
- `npx vitest run` — 9629 tests passed across 389 files.
- `cd src-tauri && cargo check --benches` — clean.
- `prek run --all-files` — pending (run at commit time).

**Process notes:**
- 4 build subagents launched in parallel (one per item). 2.5 reported in 4 minutes; 2.6 in 12 minutes; 2.11 in 21 minutes; 2.12 in 17 minutes. The longer 2.11/2.12 times reflect register-in-`agaric_commands!`-macro + specta-bindings-regen + tauri-mock-parity loops, not real complexity.
- During 2.6 + 2.12, the subagents updated their direct test files (useAppBootRecovery.test, usePropertyDefForEdit.test, block_cmd_tests) but missed two cross-cutting integration test files: `App.test.tsx` (5 tests still mocking `list_drafts`/`flush_draft`/`list_property_defs`) and `SortableBlock.test.tsx` (14 tests using a now-stale `pageOf([def])` envelope). Orchestrator fixed these directly: rewrote App's two draft-failure tests as one `flush_all_drafts`-failure test; renamed `mockListPropertyDefs` → `mockGetPropertyDef` and used a regex pass to convert all `pageOf([def])` callsites to bare `def` (11 instances). One additional fix needed for an arg-aware mock (the test seeded a `status` def but asked for `effort` — needed `mockImplementation` instead of `mockResolvedValue`).
- Single review subagent (instead of 2-3) — appropriate for a small batch where each item is independently small. PASS on all four items.

**Lessons learned (for future sessions):**
- Subagent prompts that say "update existing tests in `useAppBootRecovery.test.ts`" should also enumerate cross-cutting integration test files (`App.test.tsx`, `SortableBlock.test.tsx`) where the same IPC names appear. Otherwise the orchestrator must clean them up post-build, which works but extends the loop.
- For shape-changed mock data (paginated envelope → bare object), a one-shot regex pass is faster than per-test edits when the pattern is uniform; document the regex so a reviewer can verify the conversion was mechanical.

**Commit plan:** single commit.

## Session 688 — PEND-35 Tier 3 (4 items): indexes + SQL anti-patterns (2026-05-08)

| Metadata | Value |
|----------|-------|
| **Date** | 2026-05-08 |
| **Subagents** | 4 build + 2 review (technical only — backend-only changes, no UX surface) |
| **Items closed** | PEND-35 Tier 3.1, 3.2, 3.3, 3.4 |
| **Items modified** | PEND-35 (Tier 3 section retired; TL;DR + status header updated) |
| **Tests added** | +7 backend / +2 frontend (3.1: 1 repurposed migration test; 3.2: 1 EXPLAIN test; 3.3: 1 EXPLAIN test; 3.4: 5 backend + 2 frontend) |
| **Files touched** | 24 source files + 3 new migrations + 4 .sqlx cache delta |

**Summary:** Closed all four Tier 3 (indexes / SQL anti-patterns) findings from the PEND-35 audit. Three migrations land: 0048 retires the redundant op_log expression index from migration 0003 (now that 4 query sites read the denormalized `op_log.block_id` column added in migration 0030); 0049 adds a partial index `idx_blocks_conflict` for the conflict-listing query; 0050 adds `idx_tags_cache_name_nocase` so every tag-picker keystroke can index-serve the case-insensitive LIKE-prefix query. The fourth item expands `query_by_property` (block_type + value_text_in via json_each + value_date_range half-open) and `query_by_tags`/`eval_tag_query` (block_type) — the `query_by_property` Tauri command bundles the Tier 1.5 + Tier 3.4 filters into an `ExtraQueryFilters` struct to stay under specta's 10-arg ceiling, mirror-pattern of `AgendaQuery`. The flat FE wrapper API is preserved. These signature expansions are the prerequisite for Tier 2.8/2.9/2.10 callsite cleanups in a future batch.

**REVIEW-LATER impact:**
- **Top-level open count:** Unchanged (PEND-35 audit, not REVIEW-LATER)
- **Previously resolved:** Unchanged

**Files touched (this session):**
- `src-tauri/migrations/0048_drop_op_log_payload_block_id_index.sql` (new) — `DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_op_log_payload_block_id;`
- `src-tauri/migrations/0049_index_blocks_conflict.sql` (new) — partial index on `(id) WHERE is_conflict = 1 AND deleted_at IS NULL`
- `src-tauri/migrations/0050_index_tags_cache_name_nocase.sql` (new) — `idx_tags_cache_name_nocase ON tags_cache(name COLLATE NOCASE)`
- `src-tauri/src/pagination/history.rs` — 2 sites: `json_extract(ol.payload, '$.block_id')` → `ol.block_id`
- `src-tauri/src/commands/history.rs` — 2 sites: `o.payload` and `ol.payload` aliases
- `src-tauri/src/op_log.rs` — repurposed test confirms migration 0048 dropped the legacy index AND `idx_op_log_block_id` is intact
- `src-tauri/src/pagination/properties.rs` — `query_by_property` gains `block_type` / `value_text_in` / `value_date_range` on both reserved-key and non-reserved-key branches; `value_text_in` / `value_text` mutual-exclusion validation
- `src-tauri/src/tag_query/query.rs` — `eval_tag_query` gains `block_type`; new `list_tags_by_prefix_uses_nocase_index` test
- `src-tauri/src/commands/queries.rs` — `query_by_property_inner` + Tauri wrapper; `ExtraQueryFilters` IPC bundle
- `src-tauri/src/commands/tags.rs` — `query_by_tags_inner` + wrapper gain `block_type`
- `src-tauri/src/commands/mod.rs` — new `ExtraQueryFilters` struct (specta `Type, Serialize, Deserialize, Default` derivations, `serde(rename_all = "camelCase")`)
- `src-tauri/src/pagination/tests.rs` — new `list_conflicts_uses_partial_index` EXPLAIN test; existing call-sites updated to new arity
- `src-tauri/src/commands/tests/{query,tag}_cmd_tests.rs`, `src-tauri/src/command_integration_tests/property_integration.rs`, `src-tauri/src/integration_tests.rs` — call-site updates + 5 new query_by_property/query_by_tags tests
- `src-tauri/benches/tag_query_bench.rs` — call-site updates (5 sites)
- `src/lib/tauri.ts` — wrappers expose flat API for `queryByProperty` / `queryByTags`; bundle into `extraFilters` at IPC boundary
- `src/lib/bindings.ts` — regenerated by specta
- `src/lib/tauri-mock/handlers.ts` — mocks honour all five extraFilters knobs + blockType on query_by_tags (parity with backend half-open date range and json_each set membership)
- `src/components/__tests__/{QueryResult,TagFilterPanel}.test.tsx`, `src/lib/__tests__/{tauri,agenda-filters,template-utils}.test.ts` — call-site updates + 2 new FE round-trip tests
- `src-tauri/.sqlx/` — 3 entries deleted (old json_extract shapes from 3.1), 4 added (new `ol.block_id IN` shapes — one extra appears to come from a related query path)
- `pending/PEND-35-tauri-command-backend-vs-frontend-audit.md` — Tier 3 section retired; TL;DR + status header updated

**Verification:**
- `cd src-tauri && cargo nextest run` — 3661 tests run, 3661 passed (+7 from baseline 3654 after Tier 1).
- `npx vitest run` — 9601 tests passed across 386 files.
- `cd src-tauri && cargo check --benches` — clean.
- `prek run --all-files` — pending (run at commit time).

**Process notes:**
- Four build subagents launched in parallel by item (3.1 / 3.2 / 3.3 / 3.4). Migration numbers 0048/0049/0050 pre-allocated by orchestrator to avoid collision. 3.1, 3.2, 3.3 reported in 5-7 minutes each; 3.4 took ~25 minutes because it had to propagate signature changes through `tag_query/query.rs`, `commands/tags.rs`, three integration test files, and the `tag_query_bench.rs` (signature was 5-arg, now 6-arg in `eval_tag_query` + 8→9-arg in `query_by_tags_inner`).
- Two review subagents (no UX review needed — Tier 3 is backend-only). T1 (3.1+3.2+3.3) FIX-APPLIED `IF NOT EXISTS` to migrations 0049/0050 for consistency with the rest of the migration corpus. T2 (3.4) PASS — no fixes, but spot-checked the half-open date range with mutation testing to confirm the test is load-bearing.

**Lessons learned (for future sessions):**
- When adding a new IPC arg pushes the wrapper past specta's 10-arg ceiling, bundle into a struct rather than splitting commands — `AgendaQuery` is the canonical precedent. Folding adjacent recently-added knobs into the same bundle (Tier 1.5's `excludeParentId`/`contentNonEmpty` here) is fine and keeps headroom for future filters.
- For migration-only batches, a single `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN`-asserts-index-name test per index is enough verification — but ONLY if the test SQL byte-mirrors the production SQL. Drift between test SQL and production SQL silently invalidates the assertion. Worth establishing a helper that pulls the SQL from a constant shared between production code and test, or at minimum a code comment cross-referencing the production line numbers.

**Commit plan:** single commit.
